## `AFG-1393-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Tuft-Withdrawal Tester
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Carpet Tuft Bind Force, Wear Abrasion & Flame Retardancy
